Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The segmentation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 860. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 663 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_860(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 860. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^663(S, F) is trivial. QED.
